# 16 (annex). Test-Case Registry

## Purpose

Every test-case identifier in the kit, the **one** document that defines it, what it proves, and every document that cites it. ADR-0020 sets the rule: a test is defined once and cited everywhere else. This annex is generated by `node tools/plan-build/gen-tc-registry.mjs` from the same code as kit-lint rule R20, which fails the lint on a second definition or a citation with none; editing it by hand is a defect. Document 16 owns the test strategy; this annex owns nothing but the list.

## Scope

| In scope | Owned elsewhere |
|---|---|
| Every `TC-` identifier in `docs/brief` and `docs/plan`, its owner, its meaning and its citations | The test itself: its owner document |
| The derived acceptance tests of document 20 Section 2 | The requirement that each one proves: document 03 |

## Content

### 1. Summary

| Measure | Value |
|---|---|
| Test cases defined in one document | 1566 |
| Derived acceptance tests (950 to 999), owned by their requirement | 334 |
| Identifiers defined in two documents or cited with no definition | 0 |
| Areas | 36 |

### 2. How ownership is decided

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Defined | A table cell holds the identifier and nothing else, in the first column or in a column headed Test, Test case, TC, Identifier, Test case ID or Test id (Appendix W's Demo column included) |
| Cited | Any other appearance. A cell such as "TC-ATT-003 (Appendix R)" is a citation by design |
| Precedence when two documents want one test | Appendix R, then Appendix W, then the service sheet of the identifier's area, then the area's cross-cutting plan document, then any other document |
| Same identifier, different tests | The lower-precedence document renumbers |
| Derived acceptance test | `TC-<AREA>-(950 + requirement number)`, owned by the requirement (document 20 Section 2) |

## Decisions in force

| Decision | Record |
|---|---|
| `TC-<AREA>-<NNN>` is the unit of proof | ADR-0014 |
| One definition per test case, citations name the owner, precedence as above | ADR-0020 |
| This annex is generated, never edited by hand | ADR-0020 |

| Claim | Proof |
